14 Oct 2026 05:40:19,061 [10952] INFO  Log - ExecuteQuery(
            SELECT DISTINCT ab.EmployeeID,
                   dbo.FN_DESC('AddEmployee',ab.EmployeeID) AS empname
            FROM AddEmployee ab
            JOIN AssginBussiness asb ON ab.EmployeeID=asb.EmployeeID
            OUTER APPLY (
                SELECT TOP(1) s.mp_Stauts
                FROM Emp_Active_Inactive_Status s
                WHERE s.EmpId=ab.EmployeeID
                  AND s.Effective_Date<=CONVERT(date,GETDATE())
                ORDER BY s.Effective_Date DESC,
                         TRY_CONVERT(datetime2,s.Datetime_Stamp) DESC,
                         s.Datetime_Stamp DESC
            ) hist
            WHERE asb.UserTypeId=2
              AND asb.BusinessID=2078
              AND COALESCE(hist.mp_Stauts,
                    NULLIF(ab.Employee_Status,''),
                    CASE WHEN UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(ab.IsActive)))='YES' THEN 'Active' ELSE 'Not Active' END)='Active'
            ORDER BY empname) Start

14 Oct 2026 05:47:41,097 [8872] INFO  Log - ExecuteQuery(
            SELECT DISTINCT ab.EmployeeID,
                   dbo.FN_DESC('AddEmployee',ab.EmployeeID) AS empname
            FROM AddEmployee ab
            JOIN AssginBussiness asb ON ab.EmployeeID=asb.EmployeeID
            OUTER APPLY (
                SELECT TOP(1) s.mp_Stauts
                FROM Emp_Active_Inactive_Status s
                WHERE s.EmpId=ab.EmployeeID
                  AND s.Effective_Date<=CONVERT(date,GETDATE())
                ORDER BY s.Effective_Date DESC,
                         TRY_CONVERT(datetime2,s.Datetime_Stamp) DESC,
                         s.Datetime_Stamp DESC
            ) hist
            WHERE asb.UserTypeId=2
              AND asb.BusinessID=2079
              AND COALESCE(hist.mp_Stauts,
                    NULLIF(ab.Employee_Status,''),
                    CASE WHEN UPPER(LTRIM(RTRIM(ab.IsActive)))='YES' THEN 'Active' ELSE 'Not Active' END)='Active'
            ORDER BY empname) Start
